# Resources by calendar week

Generated 2026-08-24. Ranks 1807 scraped resources against each calendar week's theme, hooks and outlines using BM25 over the resource's title, description, tags and full scraped text where available. Top 12 matches per week, floor score 1.0, each resource allocated to at most 2 of its best-fit weeks so broad-vocabulary pages (blog indexes, course landing pages) do not recur in every week's list.

Source corpus: `mlsource/data/all_resources.json` + `mlsource/resources/content/`. Regenerate with `python3 tools/build_topic_index.py` whenever the calendar or the corpus changes; this file is a derived artifact, not hand-edited.

### Week 21: System Design Fundamentals - Builder's Pass, part 1

- [System Design Simulator](https://systemdesignsimulator.org/) _( web | systemdesignsimulator.org )_
  Explore system design and database internals through interactive simulations, failures, and stress tests.
- [Database Internals](https://www.databass.dev/) _( web | databass.dev )_
  Deep dive into storage engines, B-trees, LSM trees, and distributed database algorithms.
- [Arpit Bhayani](https://arpitbhayani.me/blogs) _( web | arpitbhayani.me )_
  Weekly essays on system design, distributed systems, database internals, and algorithms.
- [In Search of an Understandable Consensus Algorithm](https://raft.github.io/raft.pdf) _( pdf | raft.github.io )_
  Raft is the consensus paper to read before discussing leader election, replicated logs, or metadata coordination. It is intentionally structured for understandability, which makes it a strong beginner systems paper.
- [Raft Consensus Visualization](https://raft.github.io/) _( web | raft.github.io )_
  Manipulate a live Raft cluster and watch leader elections and replicated logs evolve.
- [Designing Data-Intensive Applications](https://dataintensive.net/) _( web | dataintensive.net )_
  The definitive guide to data systems , replication, partitioning, transactions, and distributed consensus.
- [CockroachDB](https://www.cockroachlabs.com/) _( web | cockroachlabs.com )_
